<OperationCodeSamplesSection slot="code-samples">

<CodeSampleTabbedSection>

<CodeSampleTab
  slot="tab"
  id="operation-list_pipelines_v1_observability_pipelines_get_code-samples_typescript"
  tags={{"codeSample":"typescript"}}
>

TypeScript

</CodeSampleTab>

<SectionContent
  slot="content"
  id="operation-list_pipelines_v1_observability_pipelines_get_code-samples_typescript"
>

<CodeSample>

```typescript
import { Mistral } from "@mistralai/mistralai";

const mistral = new Mistral({
  apiKey: "MISTRAL_API_KEY",
});

async function run() {
  const result = await mistral.beta.observability.evaluations.listPipelines({});

  console.log(result);
}

run();

```

</CodeSample>

</SectionContent>

<CodeSampleTab
  slot="tab"
  id="operation-list_pipelines_v1_observability_pipelines_get_code-samples_python"
  tags={{"codeSample":"python"}}
>

Python

</CodeSampleTab>

<SectionContent
  slot="content"
  id="operation-list_pipelines_v1_observability_pipelines_get_code-samples_python"
>

<SDKVersionCodeSample>
<div data-version="v2">
<CodeSample>

```python
from mistralai.client import Mistral
import os


with Mistral(
    api_key=os.getenv("MISTRAL_API_KEY", ""),
) as mistral:

    res = mistral.beta.observability.evaluations.list_pipelines(page_size=50, page=1)

    # Handle response
    print(res)


```

</CodeSample>
</div>

</SDKVersionCodeSample>

</SectionContent>

Create a pipeline

<OperationCodeSamplesSection slot="code-samples">

<CodeSampleTab
  slot="tab"

>

TypeScript

</CodeSampleTab>

<SectionContent
  slot="content"
  id="operation-create_pipeline_v1_observability_pipelines_post_code-samples_typescript"
>

<CodeSample>

```typescript
import { Mistral } from "@mistralai/mistralai";

const mistral = new Mistral({
  apiKey: "MISTRAL_API_KEY",
});

async function run() {
  const result = await mistral.beta.observability.evaluations.createPipeline({
    name: "<value>",
    selectors: [],
    definitions: [
      {
        slug: "<value>",
      },
    ],
  });

  console.log(result);
}

run();

```

</CodeSample>

</SectionContent>

<CodeSampleTab
  slot="tab"
  id="operation-create_pipeline_v1_observability_pipelines_post_code-samples_python"
  tags={{"codeSample":"python"}}
>

Python

</CodeSampleTab>

<SectionContent
  slot="content"
  id="operation-create_pipeline_v1_observability_pipelines_post_code-samples_python"
>

<SDKVersionCodeSample>
<div data-version="v2">
<CodeSample>

```python
from mistralai.client import Mistral
import os


with Mistral(
    api_key=os.getenv("MISTRAL_API_KEY", ""),
) as mistral:

    res = mistral.beta.observability.evaluations.create_pipeline(name="<value>", selectors=[], definitions=[
        {
            "slug": "<value>",
        },
    ], enabled=True)

    # Handle response
    print(res)


```

</CodeSample>
</div>

</SDKVersionCodeSample>

</SectionContent>

<CodeSample>

```curl
curl https://api.mistral.ai/v1/observability/pipelines \
 -X POST \
 -H 'Authorization: Bearer YOUR_APIKEY_HERE' \
 -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
 -d '{
  "definitions": [
    {
      "slug": "ipsum eiusmod"
    }
  ],
  "name": "My resource",
  "selectors": [
    {
      "source_kind": "span"
    }
  ]
}'
```

_Get a pipeline_

Get a pipeline

<OperationCodeSamplesSection slot="code-samples">

<CodeSampleTabbedSection>

<CodeSampleTab
  slot="tab"
  id="operation-get_pipeline_v1_observability_pipelines_pipeline_id_get_code-samples_typescript"
  tags={{"codeSample":"typescript"}}
>

TypeScript

</CodeSampleTab>

<SectionContent
  slot="content"
  id="operation-get_pipeline_v1_observability_pipelines_pipeline_id_get_code-samples_typescript"
>

<CodeSample>

```typescript
import { Mistral } from "@mistralai/mistralai";

const mistral = new Mistral({
  apiKey: "MISTRAL_API_KEY",
});

async function run() {
  const result = await mistral.beta.observability.evaluations.getPipeline({
    pipelineId: "aafed470-377a-4e11-85ec-e5de5b40c79f",
  });

  console.log(result);
}

run();

```

</CodeSample>

</SectionContent>

<CodeSampleTab
  slot="tab"
  id="operation-get_pipeline_v1_observability_pipelines_pipeline_id_get_code-samples_python"
  tags={{"codeSample":"python"}}
>

Python

</CodeSampleTab>

<SectionContent
  slot="content"
  id="operation-get_pipeline_v1_observability_pipelines_pipeline_id_get_code-samples_python"
>

<SDKVersionCodeSample>
<div data-version="v2">
<CodeSample>

```python
from mistralai.client import Mistral
import os


with Mistral(
    api_key=os.getenv("MISTRAL_API_KEY", ""),
) as mistral:

    res = mistral.beta.observability.evaluations.get_pipeline(pipeline_id="aafed470-377a-4e11-85ec-e5de5b40c79f")

    # Handle response
    print(res)


```

</CodeSample>
</div>

</SDKVersionCodeSample>

<CodeSample>

```typescript
import { Mistral } from "@mistralai/mistralai";

const mistral = new Mistral({
  apiKey: "MISTRAL_API_KEY",
});

async function run() {
  const result = await mistral.beta.observability.evaluations.updatePipeline({
    pipelineId: "99632c18-966b-48d2-8d1f-b02c9a9bf9df",
    updatePipelineRequest: {},
  });

  console.log(result);
}

run();

```

<CodeSample>

```python
from mistralai.client import Mistral
import os


with Mistral(
    api_key=os.getenv("MISTRAL_API_KEY", ""),
) as mistral:

    res = mistral.beta.observability.evaluations.update_pipeline(pipeline_id="2c18966b-8d2d-41fb-a02c-9a9bf9df2919")

    # Handle response
    print(res)


```

## <Pill variant="primary"><b>DELETE</b></Pill> /v1/observability/pipelines/\{pipeline\_id\} \{#operation-delete_pipeline_v1_observability_pipelines_pipeline_id_delete\}

>

<CodeSample>

```typescript
import { Mistral } from "@mistralai/mistralai";

const mistral = new Mistral({
  apiKey: "MISTRAL_API_KEY",
});

async function run() {
  const result = await mistral.beta.observability.evaluations.get({
    evaluationSlug: "<value>",
  });

  console.log(result);
}

run();

```

</CodeSample>

<SDKVersionCodeSample>
<div data-version="v2">
<CodeSample>

```python
from mistralai.client import Mistral
import os


with Mistral(
    api_key=os.getenv("MISTRAL_API_KEY", ""),
) as mistral:

    res = mistral.beta.observability.evaluations.get(evaluation_slug="<value>")

    # Handle response
    print(res)


```

</CodeSample>
